No separate lemma is assumed as an accepted premise; the required arguments and test input are reproduced below.\n\n## 1. Original coordinates, counts, and the fixed child\n\nNormalize smoothness to1. For any word w=(w_0,...,w_(n-1)), write\nS_w=sum w_i, A_w=1+S_w, and retain all n+1 formal gradients g_i and values\nf_i. Put x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)w_t g_t and\n\n Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,\n V_w=2sum_(i<n)w_i(f_i-f_n)+sum_(i<n)w_i(1-w_i)||g_i||^2\n                                      -S_w(S_w+1)||g_n||^2.\n\nLet e be the all-ones column, T_ii=1, T_it=w_t for t<i, T_it=0 for t>i,\nomega=(-2w_0,...,-2w_(n-1),2S_w)^T, and\n\n D=diag(w_0(2-w_0),...,w_(n-1)(2-w_(n-1)),-S_w(S_w+2)).\n\nA full certificate consists of Lambda_ii=0, Lambda_ij>=0 for every\nordered i!=j, L=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda, L^T e=omega, and\n\n M=D+(LT+T^T L^T)/2 positive semidefinite.                    (1)\n\nHere sym(B)=(B+B^T)/2; Gram is the matrix of pairwise gradient inner\nproducts, and the matrix pairing in (2) is trace(M^T Gram).\n\nThe accepted original-coordinate identity is\n\n V_w+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij=<M,Gram(g_0,...,g_n)>.          (2)\n\nIt holds as a polynomial in every formal gradient and value, without\nidentifying coordinates. The signs Q_ij<=0 hold on an actual trajectory\nof a single convex differentiable1-smooth function. Consequently (1)\nis a sufficient primitive certificate on all such trajectories.\n\nIn this manuscript only, set a=sqrt(2), C=1+a, d=a-1=1/C. The fixed child\nis q=(a). Its two-node certificate is\n\n Lambda^q=[[0,2],[2C,0]], M^q=0.                             (3)\n\nIndeed its flow is (-2a,2a). The diagonal entries of D+sym(LT) are\na(2-a)+2(1-a)=2-a^2=0 and -a(a+2)+2C=0. Twice the off-diagonal is\n-2+2C(a-1)=0. Thus every entry of the full matrix is checked.\n\nThe arbitrary input h has length r>=1, h_i>=1, capacity A=1+sum h_i,\nand its given full certificate (Lambda^h,M^h). Define\n\n beta_i=1+C(h_i-1)=C h_i-a,\n H=(a,beta_0,a,beta_1,...,a,beta_(r-1),a).                   (4)\n\nBlock i has physical node2i (its start) and node2i+1 (its endpoint),\nfor i=0,...,r. All 2r+2 gradients remain independent formal coordinates.\nThere are r+1 child steps and r bridges, so the physical length is\nNphys=2r+1 and there are (2r+2)(2r+1) ordered multiplier slots. Since beta_i>=1,\nevery step is positive. Direct summation, using C-a=1, gives\n\n 1+sum H=1+(r+1)a+C sum h_i-r a=C A.                       (5)\n\nThis is a fixed-child statement only. Neither arbitrary-child closure\nnor an exponent-based restriction on arbitrary seed capacity follows.\n\n## 2. A nonbasic legal input, with its complete certificate\n\nTake h=(3/2,3/2), A=4, and\n\n Lambda^h=[[0,1,1/2],[7/2,0,17/2],[1,14,0]].                (6)\n\nIts six ordered entries are positive and its flow is (-3,-3,6).\nWith T=[[1,0,0],[3/2,1,0],[3/2,3/2,1]] and D=diag(3/4,3/4,-15),\nthe three diagonal entries of LT are (-3/4,-3/4,15). The sums of its\nopposite off-diagonal entries are\n\n (LT)_01+(LT)_10=(-1-3/4)+7/4=0,\n (LT)_02+(LT)_20=-1/2+1/2=0,\n (LT)_12+(LT)_21=-17/2+(3/2+7)=0.\n\nHence the full M^h is zero. A length2 primitive binary tree built from\nempty leaves contains a length1 empty-leaf join, whose step is a by the\naccepted join equation. Neither step in (6) equals a. Thus this is a\nnonbasic input for the present test, with all its certificate data checked.\n\nIts physical inflated word is\n\n H=(a,b,a,b,a), b=(3+a)/2, capacity4C.                       (7)\n\nThe unit-quadratic trajectory from x_0=1 has scalar gradient column\n\n v=(1,-d,1/2,-d/2,1/4,-d/4)^T.                            (8)\n\nIn particular its primitive energy is zero: (4C)^2 v_5^2=1.\nEquation(8) will test candidate remainder matrices; changing a coordinate\nof (8) does NOT automatically give another actual function trajectory.\n\n## 4. Mechanism2: preserve the input PSD remainder, solve real equalities\n\nThis mechanism starts from an arbitrary full input certificate, with\nno zero-remainder, symmetry, support, rank, or strict-positivity assumption.\nIts canonical real output will fail a sign below. The exact remaining\nfreedoms are then retained, rather than silently fixed to that output.\n\n### 4.1 Two necessary moments, derived in the original coordinates\n\nFor any word w define its unit-curvature column by\n\n v^w_0=1, v^w_i=product_(t<i)(1-w_t), so T_w v^w=e.\n\nFor any array with exact flow, (2) on the formal constant-gradient data\ng_i=1, x_i=-sum_(t<i)w_t, f_i=x_i gives e^T M e=0. Indeed every Q is0,\nand 2sum w_i(S_w-sum_(t<i)w_t)=S_w^2+sum w_i^2 cancels the remaining\nterms in V_w. If M is PSD, this equality forces M e=0.\n\nOn the actual unit quadratic from x_0=1 all Q are0 and the gradient\ncolumn is v^w. The telescope\n\n sum_(i<n)w_i(2-w_i)(v^w_i)^2=1-(v^w_n)^2\n\ngives\n\n (v^w)^T M v^w=(v^w)^T D_w v^w\n                =1-A_w^2(v^w_n)^2=:chi_w.                 (20)\n\nThese identities are necessary even when coefficients are on the\nboundary or M is nonzero and singular. They do not identify formal\ngradients or impose restrictions on an actual function.\n\n### 4.2 The fixed-child pullback preserves both moments exactly\n\nLet v^h be the input column and v=v^H the full physical column. The\nproduct of the two successive factors through block i and its bridge is\n\n (1-a)(1-beta_i)=(-d)C(1-h_i)=-(1-h_i).\n\nIt follows, without division by any 1-h_i, that\n\n v_(2i)=(-1)^i v^h_i,\n v_(2i+1)=-d(-1)^i v^h_i.                                (21)\n\nTogether with (5), this proves chi_H=chi_h. Define a linear map Rpull\nfrom all physical gradient coordinates to r+1 coordinates by\n\n (Rpull z)_i=z_(2i)                         if i is even,\n (Rpull z)_i=-d z_(2i)+a z_(2i+1)            if i is odd.   (22)\n\nHere z is any formal scalar coordinate column; the same map acts\ncomponentwise on vector gradients. Since a-d=1 and dC=1,\n\n Rpull e_H=e_h,  Rpull v=v^h.\n\nConsequently the explicit full physical matrix\n\n N0=Rpull^T M^h Rpull                                      (23)\n\nis PSD, satisfies N0 e_H=0 and v^T N0 v=chi_H, and is defined for every\nallowed input certificate. Equations(22)--(23) are a congruence of\nmatrices on the full physical space, not a smooth virtual-oracle claim.\nThe map contains a negative coefficient on odd blocks; no entrywise\npositivity is claimed for the multiplier array reconstructed from it.\n\n### 4.3 Explicit real skew completion for all compatible remainders\n\nIn this subsection use the physical word H, let m=2r+2, and write\nT=T_H,D=D_H,omega=omega_H,e=e_H,v=v^H. The vectors e and v are independent:\ntheir first two coordinates are respectively (1,1) and (1,-d).\n\nTake any symmetric matrix N with\n\n N e=0, v^T N v=chi_H.                                    (24)\n\nIt need not be PSD for the algebra below. Define vectors and scalars\n\n bvec=-D e-T^T omega,       cvec=(D-N)v,\n alpha=(e^T v)/m,          z=v-alpha e,       ell=z^T z>0,\n uvec=cvec-alpha bvec+e(bvec^T z)/m.\n\nThe identities needed for a skew completion are\n\n e^T bvec=0, v^T cvec=0, e^T cvec=-v^T bvec.               (25)\n\nFor the first, writing P_i=sum_(t<i)H_t, direct expansion gives\n(bvec)_i=P_i^2-P_(i+1)^2 for i<Nphys, and (bvec)_Nphys=(sum H)^2;\nthe sum is zero. The second follows from (20),(24). The third follows\nfrom N e=0, T v=e and e^T omega=0. These also give\ne^T uvec=z^T uvec=0.\n\nSet\n\n K_N=(bvec e^T-e bvec^T)/m\n              +(uvec z^T-z uvec^T)/ell.                   (26)\n\nThis is an explicit skew matrix. Its actions are\nK_N e=bvec and K_N z=cvec-alpha bvec, hence K_N v=cvec.\nFor EVERY real skew matrix Z with\n\n Z e=0, Z v=0,                                            (27)\n\nput\n\n L_N,Z=(N-D+K_N+Z)T^(-1),\n Lambda_N,Z(i,j)=-(L_N,Z)_ij for i!=j, with diagonal0.      (28)\n\nThen L_N,Z e=0, since (N-D)v+K_N v=0. Also\n\n L_N,Z^T e=T^(-T)(-D e-bvec)=omega,\n D+sym(L_N,Z T)=N.                                       (29)\n\nIts zero row sums show L_N,Z=diag(Lambda_N,Z e)-Lambda_N,Z.\nThus every real coefficient equality in (1)--(2) holds identically on\nthe entire physical space. If N is PSD, the remainder is PSD. The only\nconditions still to check are all original multiplier signs.\n\nNo denominator depends on a step minus1. The inverse in (28) can be\nwritten without matrix solving: (T^(-1))_ij is0 for i<j,1 for i=j, and\n\n -H_j product_(j<t<i)(1-H_t) for i>j.                      (30)\n\nFor example, multiplying its column j by T cancels successive partial\nproducts, proving (30), including any zero factor. All cases h_i=1 and\nsingular input M are therefore retained.\n\nConversely suppose a full physical certificate is given, with remainder N.\nSection4.1 gives (24). Its skew part K=LT-(N-D) has K e=bvec and K v=cvec.\nThus K-K_N is exactly a matrix Z of (27), and (28) recovers the original\narray. There is no assumption of a unique certificate or unique skew part.\n\n### 4.4 Exact full residual, with unrestricted homogeneous freedom\n\nRelative to the explicit input-dependent base N0 of (23), allow ANY\nsymmetric matrix Delta satisfying\n\n Delta e=0, v^T Delta v=0, N0+Delta PSD.                   (31)\n\nDelta need not itself be PSD. Allow simultaneously EVERY Z of (27).\nThen full physical feasibility is equivalent to existence of such\n(Delta,Z) for which, for every ordered i!=j,\n\n [(N0+Delta-D+K_(N0+Delta)+Z)T^(-1)]_ij <=0.               (32)\n\nSufficiency follows from (28)--(29). Necessity follows from the converse\nand Delta=N-N0, since every compatible physical PSD N has the same\nmoment(20). Thus (31)--(32) discard no possible physical certificate.\nThey preserve arbitrary nonsymmetric multipliers, every physical query,\nevery homogeneous skew degree of freedom and every admissible full PSD\nremainder. They are an exact residual sign-completion problem, not a\nproof that its solution exists for every input.\n\nIn particular, setting Delta=Z=0 is a concrete canonical attempted lift.\nIt always has full PSD and exact real flow, but need not have nonnegative\nmultipliers. Restricting to Delta=0, to Delta PSD, or to Z=0 would be an\nadditional sufficient-class restriction, not an established reduction.\n\n## 5. The canonical PSD/skew lift fails a sign on the nonbasic input\n\nUse (6)--(8). Because M^h=0, the canonical choice N=N0=0 is a full PSD\nremainder. It remains to test the actual ordered signs of (28), Z=0.\nAll data for the following calculation are supplied explicitly:\n\n m=6,\n D=diag(2d,(1-2a)/4,2d,(1-2a)/4,2d,1-16C^2),\n bvec=(-2,(-19-18a)/4,-8-3a,(-49-42a)/4,-14-6a,41+24a)^T,\n cvec=(2d,(5-3a)/4,d,(5-3a)/8,d/2,(17+15a)/4)^T,\n alpha=7(2-a)/24,\n v^T v=21(2-a)/8,\n ell=7(2-a)(22+7a)/96,\n bvec^T v=-(21+49a)/8.\n\nThe entries of bvec can also be checked by the squared-prefix telescope\nin Section4.3; cvec=Dv. These checks require only a^2=2.\n\nFor an explicit audit of the needed entry of (26), put\nzeta=(bvec^T v)/6=-(21+49a)/48. Eliminating alpha^2 from (26) gives\n\n ell (K_0)_01\n =(bvec_0-bvec_1)(v^T v)/6+(cvec_0 v_1-v_0 cvec_1)\n   +alpha(cvec_1-cvec_0-bvec_0 v_1+v_0 bvec_1)\n   +zeta(v_1-v_0).\n\nThe four summands are respectively\n\n (-98+175a)/64,\n (-29+19a)/4,\n (546-532a)/96,\n (98+21a)/48.\n\nTheir sum is (-202+457a)/192. Therefore\n\n (K_0)_01=(-202+457a)/(192 ell)>0.                          (33)\n\nFor the first column of T^(-1), formula(30) gives\n\n T^(-1)e0=(2+a)v-C e0.\n\nSince (-D+K_0)v=0 and D has no off-diagonal entries, (28) now gives\n\n Lambda_0,0(1,0)=-C(K_0)_01\n                =-C(-202+457a)/(192 ell)<0.                (34)\n\nThe first subscript0 in Lambda_0,0 specifies N=0 and the second Z=0;\n(1,0) is the physical ordered pair. Equation(34) is a negative ordered\nmultiplier in the exact real, zero-remainder array produced by the\ncanonical completion. Thus this second mechanism also fails its first\nnonbasic positivity test when its free variables are set to zero.\n\nThis failure cannot be attributed to a missing common-kernel equality,\nunit-curvature moment, or PSD remainder: all of them have been proved\nfor this exact array. Its unresolved issue is precisely coefficient\nnonnegativity, with the full correction variables(31)--(32) still available.\n\nOn this saturated input, chi_H=0, so any feasible output PSD remainder\nmust annihilate both e and v. Since N0=0 here, (31) means Delta PSD with\nDelta e=Delta v=0. This is a derived condition for this particular test,\nnot a zero-remainder restriction on the general problem. Nonzero Delta\nand every skew Z annihilating e,v remain allowed; neither has been ruled\nout. Consequently (34) does not prove physical inflation false.\n\nThere is a more explicit form of the unpaid correction on this example.\nBecause Delta v=0, the canonical skew K_(Delta) in (26) remains K_0.\nThe first-column calculation above then makes the ordered sign(1,0)\nequivalent to the necessary inequality\n\n Delta_10+Z_10 >= (K_0)_01.                                (35)\n\nThis inequality alone is not impossible. For example, put\n\n pfree=(1,0,-3,0,2,0)^T,\n qfree=(d,1,-d,-1,0,0)^T.\n\nEach vector is orthogonal to both e and v: the nontrivial products are\nv^T pfree=1-3/2+2/4=0 and\nv^T qfree=d-d-d/2+d/2=0. Hence\n\n Z=(K_0)_01(qfree pfree^T-pfree qfree^T)\n\nis an admissible homogeneous skew matrix with Z_10=(K_0)_01. With Delta=0\nit repairs exactly the displayed bad entry to zero, while preserving all\nreal equalities and the full zero remainder. The other ordered signs\nchange and are not established. {"id": "3dd2fcd7c187b9bd", "timestamp_utc": "2026-10-01T06:41:46.194702+00:00", "author": "max2", "kind": "proof_attempt", "claim": "max2 round48: complete full seven-step first-order cone has an explicit reconstructed Q(sqrt2) dual candidate proving sum p<=0, with all56 Y entries,36 original signs and full R8 PSD retained. All63 exact stationarity residuals vanish, all36 sign prices are positive, and exact dual LDL is positive definite. This JOINT UNVERIFIED result excludes every positive-capacity lifted tangent, including the contact-surviving p_test; it is not a neighborhood/global theorem or an accepted fact.", "evidence": "# Full seven-step tangent discovery: exact dual candidate\n\n**Status: complete joint UNVERIFIED research report.** All five authorized\njobs are consumed and the ledger is closed. There was no fact submission,\nformal verification, installation, benchmark replay or subagent. The original\nunrestricted gradient-descent exact-profile problem remains OPEN.\n\nThe useful output is an explicit dual over Q(sqrt(2)) for the COMPLETE\nfirst-order cone at the frozen balanced seven-step word. The recorded exact\nreconstruction satisfies all63 stationarity equations, all36 sign-price\ninequalities, and an exact positive-definite LDL decomposition. It gives\nthe following candidate conclusion:\n\n> Every full linearized certificate (p,Y,W) satisfies sum_i p_i <= 0.\n> This conclusion covers all seven physical derivatives, all56 original\n> coefficient derivatives, all36 original active signs and full R8 PSD.\n\nThis is a proof candidate with explicit algebraic evidence, not an accepted\nfact. No full-neighborhood, global seven-step or original-target theorem is\nclaimed. In particular the discovery supplies no improving finite seed.\n\n## 1. Inputs, exact model and scope\n\nThe active assignment is TASK.md, ONE round after completed47, completed\nceiling48/attempted49, under guidance06cd713a43322da8. The shared preceding\ndraft b937bb05ba1645b1 has SHA256\n76a363742a60cdab32d14b828dcf7ecd58d784dfcededfd1b9510b131d67565b.\nIt identifies this exact cone but does not solve it. Its base uniqueness,\ncontact and integration claims are not imported as accepted facts here.\n\nAccepted04219554e41d9750, Sections10--11, supplies the original full\nprimitive identity and composition. Accepted1252b8823bb5a290 fixes the\ninterpolation orientation. Accepted5e1848ee0b153f5f supplies conditional\namplification of an actual above-threshold seed only. None asserts such\na seed exists. All additional identities required below are derived jointly.\n\nLet q=sqrt(2), l=1+q, u=q-1, and\n\n    hbar=(q,2,q,1+l,q,2,q), Abar=l^3.\n\nIndex the eight independent formal gradient coordinates by0,...,7. Let\ne be their all-ones column and let e_i be the standard coordinate columns.\nFor any zero-diagonal8-by8 array X, put L(X)=diag(Xe)-X. Define T0 to have\nunit diagonal, entry hbar_j at i,j when j<i, and zero entries above its\ndiagonal. The base array has these twenty positive entries, with every\nunlisted entry zero:\n\n    01:2                  10:2l                 13:2l\n    21:2lq                23:2l^2+2lq           31:2l\n    32:2l^3               37:2l^2               43:2lq\n    45:2l^2               47:2lq                53:4l\n    54:2l^3               57:2l^3+4l            63:2lq\n    65:2l^3q              67:2l^4+2l^3q+2lq\n    73:2l                 75:2l^3               76:2l^5.\n\nCall this array Lambdabar and write Lbar=L(Lambdabar). Let Z0 be the\nset of the other36 ordered distinct pairs. The complete variable array\nY has Y_ii=0 and all56 other entries independently real before constraints.\nFor p in R7 set S=sum p_i and define\n\n    Tp_ij=p_j for j<i, zero otherwise;\n    op=(-2p_0,...,-2p_6,2S);\n    D1=diag(2(1-hbar_i)p_i for i<7,-2Abar S);\n    W=D1+sym(Lbar Tp+L(Y)T0), sym(K)=(K+K^T)/2;\n    f=L(Y)^T e-op, n=We.\n\nThe full cone consists of f=0, Y_ij>=0 for ij in Z0 and W>=0 on R8.\nThe other twenty Y entries have no imposed sign. No support, rank,\nreflection, equality of formal gradients or fixed shape of W is assumed.\n\n## 2. Exact common null and lossless PSD representation\n\nFor an arbitrary word h, s=sum h_i, and full flow L^Te=(-2h,2s), put\nM=D+sym(LT), D=diag(h_i(2-h_i),-s(s+2)), with T the original prefix\nmatrix. Since the i-th entry of Te is1+sum_(j<i)h_j,\n\n    omega^T Te=s^2+sum h_i^2,\n    e^T D e=-s^2-sum h_i^2,\n\nso e^TMe=0. Along hbar+t p and Lambdabar+tY, the flow equation is\nexact whenever f=0, and differentiation gives e^TWe=0. A symmetric PSD\nmatrix with zero quadratic form at e annihilates e: its quadratic form\nat e+t z cannot be nonnegative for all real t unless z^TWe=0 for each z.\nThus n=0 is a necessary full-cone equation.\n\nLet V=[I7;-1...-1], G=V^TV=I7+11^T and B=V^TWV. Its columns span\ne-perp. When n=0, every vector is a sum of a multiple of e and a vector\nVz, so W>=0 on R8 if and only if B>=0 on R7. Moreover\n\n    W>=m(I8-ee^T/8)  iff  B>=mG.\n\nConsequently adding all8 n=0 equations and imposing this congruent PSD\ncondition loses no original solution. The common-null direction never\nreceives an epsilon perturbation. Raw readbacks contain the original\nfull W, every original flow equation, and the full null residual.\n\n## 3. Both calibrations established before job1\n\nFor the singleton physical step x, choose multipliers2 and2(1+x) on the\ntwo directed pairs. Substitution into the original two-gradient identity\ngives the full remainder\n\n    (2-x^2)(e_0-e_1)(e_0-e_1)^T.\n\nIt is nonnegative when0<=x<=q. Set x=q-t,0<=t<q. Join identical\nsingleton children using bridge1+u(1+x), then identical three-step\nchildren using bridge2+x. Their respective capacities are1+x,l(1+x),\nand l^2(1+x). Each balanced join has right multiplier factor l^2 and\nadditional interpolation factor l^2-1=2l. The accepted full join identity\ntherefore proves feasibility without a computation. The complete array\nand its derivative are specified in frozen_model.md and in the exact\ncalibration artifact.\n\nThe derivative is p=(-1,-u,-1,-1,-1,-u,-1), with S=-l^2. If\nd_ij=e_i-e_j, its exact remainder derivative is\n\n    W=2q(d01 d01^T+l^2 d23 d23^T+l^2 d45 d45^T+l^4 d67 d67^T).\n\nEvery active zero-entry derivative is zero. This is a NONZERO positive\ncalibration from an actual feasible path. Job1 recorded exact base flow,\nbase M=0, derivative flow, null, all36 signs and the displayed rank-one\nsum identity, then numerically solved the full fixed calibration.\n\nFor the excluded direction, v(h)=T(h)^(-1)e obeys v_0=1 and\nv_(i+1)=(1-h_i)v_i. Since L e=0,\n\n    v^T Mv=v^TDv=1-A(h)^2 v_7^2.\n\nAt the base M=0 and Abar^2 vbar_7^2=1. Differentiating yields\n\n    vbar^T W vbar=-2[S/l^3+l(p0+p2+p4+p6)+p1+p5+up3].\n\nThe direction p=(1,...,1) has strictly negative right side\n-2(7/l^3+4l+2+u), so it cannot satisfy full PSD for any Y. This analytic\ncontradiction was frozen before the negative numerical calibration.\n\n## 4. Frozen jobs and observations\n\nBefore any mathematical import or evaluation, frozen_model.md,\ntangent_jobs.py and run_job.py were hashed in freeze.json. The exact dual and the candidate proof\n\nJob5 rounded free coordinates once to multiples of2^-20 and solved exact\naffine equations in the field Q(q), q^2=2. All p-box prices were set to\nzero as a reconstruction ansatz. This was not a restriction of either SDP.\nNo optimization or parameter grid was used in reconstruction.\n\nThe appendix specifies two eight-coordinate vectors a,b (the first and\nlast halves of `equality_prices`), a symmetric7-by7 matrix Z through its\n28 svec entries, and36 coefficients sigma_ij. All their displayed numbers\nare exact rational pairs (r,s), meaning r+s q. Off-diagonal svec entries\nare q Z_ij, and diagonal entries are Z_ii.\n\nThe algebraic identity certified by the63 zero stationarity residuals is\n\n    -S = trace(Z B)+sum_(ij in Z0) sigma_ij Y_ij\n         -a^T f-b^T n.                                      (D)\n\nThis is an identity for EVERY p and EVERY zero-diagonal real Y, before\nany cone constraint. There is no p-normalization term because all14 box\nprices are exactly zero.\n\nHere is an explicit way to check every coefficient of(D) without a\nsolver. Set H=VZV^T and K=H-sym(b e^T). Write t_i for the transpose of\nthe i-th row of T0 and r_ij=t_i-t_j. A single unit Y_ij contributes\ne_i-e_j to f and sym(e_i r_ij^T) to W. Thus the56 required identities are\n\n    a_i-a_j-(K r_ij)_i = 0              on the20 base-positive pairs,\n    a_i-a_j-(K r_ij)_i = sigma_ij       on the36 other pairs. (Y)\n\nFor each i<7 let C_i have entry1 in column i strictly below its diagonal,\nand zero elsewhere. The unit p_i contribution to W is\n\n    N_i=2(1-hbar_i)e_i e_i^T-2Abar e_7e_7^T+sym(Lbar C_i).\n\nIts contribution to f is2e_i-2e_7. The seven remaining identities are\n\n    2(a_i-a_7)-trace(K N_i)=1, i=0,...,6.                  (P)\n\nThe appendixed numbers and q^2=2 specify all entries in(Y) and(P).\nThe reconstruction artifact records each of their63 residuals as the\nexact pair(0,0), using the equivalent unsimplified original coefficient\nmap. It retains the exact source and deterministic arithmetic procedure.\n\nAll36 sigma values are nonnegative in the real embedding q>0, and none\nis zero. The following elementary rule makes their comparisons exact:\nif r,s have the same sign, use that sign; if they have opposite signs,\nthe sign of r+s q is sign(r) times sign(r^2-2s^2). The cases r=0 or\ns=0 are immediate. The nonzero opposite-sign case cannot yield zero,\nbecause q is irrational. This is the precise rule used by the frozen\nexact arithmetic, with rational operations only.\n\nFor Z, the artifact supplies Z=L diag(d_0,...,d_6)L^T with unit lower\ntriangular L and all seven pivots d_i strictly positive under that same\nsign rule. The appendix includes L and all seven exact pairs. The LDL\nrecurrence is\n\n    d_k=Z_kk-sum_(j<k)L_kj^2 d_j,\n    L_ik=(Z_ik-sum_(j<k)L_ij L_kj d_j)/d_k, i>k.\n\nThese formulas prove that the supplied decomposition entails Z>0, with\nno floating eigenvalue inference. All pivots were tested exactly before\ndivision, and a zero pivot with nonzero remaining column would have been\nrejected. The recorded result has no zero pivot.\n\nFor a full feasible tangent, f=n=0, every Y_ij in Z0 is nonnegative and\nB>=0. If Z=L diag(d)L^T, then\ntrace(ZB)=sum_k d_k (L e_k)^T B(L e_k)>=0. Applying(D) gives -S>=0.\nThis proves the candidate first-order capacity bound from the explicit\nalgebraic checks. A zero tangent attains S=0, so the normalized nonstrict\nproblem has exact optimum0 at draft level. The strict model likewise has\noptimum0: S>=m and S<=0 imply m<=0, and the zero point with m=0 is feasible.\n\nThe same identity also gives a useful equality condition: if S=0, each\nactive Y entry is zero, and B=0 because Z is positive definite. To see\nthe latter without a spectral theorem, each nonnegative summand\n(L e_k)^T B(L e_k) must vanish; PSD then makes B annihilate the basis\nformed by the columns of L. Consequently W=0 as well. This does not\nby itself settle how a finite higher-order perturbation can behave.\n\n## 6. What this closes and what remains\n\nAt candidate level this resolves the specific missing lifted tangent\ninterface in b937bb05ba1645b1: its contact-surviving vector\np_test=(0,0,0,0,0,2,-1), with S=1, admits NO full first-order completion\nY. The obstruction is independent of the attempted W_test, any skew\nparameterization or any support choice. It applies to all W and Y in\nthe original linearized cone.\n\nThe proof does not establish a neighborhood capacity bound. The exact\nfinite remainder still contains\n\n    D2(p)+sym(L(Y)Tp),\n    D2(p)=diag(-p_0^2,...,-p_6^2,-S^2).\n\nA finite feasible perturbation may have zero first-order capacity change,\nsingular leading terms, or coefficient difference quotients not known\nto be bounded. A separate argument must control these terms and all\nnearby full certificates. The reconstructed equality condition above\nis a concrete input for such a future argument, after verification.\nThe reconstruction also records affine elimination rank27; this is\npreserved as finite algebraic telemetry, not promoted here to an\nadditional coercivity or neighborhood theorem.\n\nNo full strict positive-capacity tangent was found, so the sufficient\nintegration construction was not invoked and no finite seed parameter\nwas selected. {"id": "336be1a25a3c10bc", "timestamp_utc": "2026-10-01T06:45:01.783780+00:00", "author": "max4", "kind": "proof_attempt", "claim": "max4 round55 JOINT UNVERIFIED actual-profile result: for every enclosing m>=10, the unit free-heat majorant fails at the actual fourth suffix update, with excess 5 mu cos(theta)<E_m<9 mu cos(theta) at edges distance3 and E_m/(mu cos(theta))->6sqrt(2). Actual strict support nevertheless grows {-k,...,k} through k=4, and the free kernels through k=4 are nonnegative. Terminal-only domination and J_m remain OPEN.", "evidence": "# Actual boundary absorption defeats the free heat profile\n\nStatus: JOINT UNVERIFIED ANALYTICAL DRAFT, max4 round55, 2026-10-01.\nNo fact_submit is authorized or made. This is the one analytical profile\nattempt allocated by TASK.md and guidance3ae9deefeb364de7. It uses no numerical\nevaluation, trajectory replay, symbolic program, solver, benchmark DP,\ninstallation, consult, or subagent. Algebra below was derived textually.\n\nThe precise new result is an ACTUAL-RUN counterexample to the proposed\nunit free-heat envelope at every physical suffix query. For every enclosing\nm>=10, the envelope holds at queries0,1,2,3 and fails at query4, immediately\nafter the original transition\n\n    H_2=(t_0,t_1,t_0), followed by t_2.\n\nAt each of the two edges at distance3 from the initially positive closing\nedge, the actual positive edge value exceeds the free heat comparison by\n\n    5 mu z < E_m < 9 mu z,\n    E_m/(mu z) -> 6 sqrt(2).                          (A)\n\nThe actual active sets are exactly {-k,...,k} for k=0,...,4 in the relabeling\nbelow. The comparison kernels are also nonnegative through query4. Thus this\nfailure coexists with actual sign nesting and positive comparison profiles.\nIt is caused by the propagation of earlier boundary absorption through the\nfirst large step, not by an arbitrary state outside the run.\n\nThis does NOT refute a terminal-only free-kernel comparison, positivity of\nall complete-block kernels, an envelope with extra slack, or an actual\nwhole-block estimate. None of those is proved here. In particular no useful\nterminal upper bound or benchmark-scale lower law for J_m is obtained.\nThe fixed family and the original all-word target remain OPEN.\n\n## 1. Unchanged object and the selected quantitative envelope\n\nRetain the exact law specified in shared37ec6ea9a52bfbdf:\n\n    lambda=1+sqrt(2), beta=1-1/m, c=lambda(1+1/m), a=beta/c,\n    t_j=1+a c^j, H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,t_j,H_j),\n    d=t_(m-1)=1+beta c^(m-2),\n    N=ceil(pi sqrt(d/6)), theta=pi/(2N), mu=sin(theta)^2,\n    z=cos(theta), v_i=cos((2i+1)theta), 0<=i<N,\n    (D x)_i=x_i-x_(i+1 modulo N), F(x)=||(D x)_+||^2/8.\n\nThe declared smoothness is L=1, the original start is v, and the original\nword is H_m, always with the SAME enclosing m. No child is independently\nretuned. All vector norms below are Euclidean. Positive parts are taken\ncoordinatewise. Let X_0=-v, and apply the actual physical suffix H_(m-1)\nto this normalized suffix start. It is the positive-amplitude factor of\nthe original inherited state; the short justification is in Section2.\n\nRelabel the closing edge N-1 as edge0, original edge i as edge i+1 modulo N.\nLet y_k be D X_k in this relabeling and r_k=(y_k)_+. Put\n\n    L_e=DD^T, P_h=I-(h/4)L_e,\n    ell=2^(m-1)-1, h=(h_0,...,h_(ell-1))=H_(m-1),\n    K_0=e_0, K_(k+1)=P_(h_k) K_k, R_k=2z K_k.        (1)\n\nHere e_i is the edge-coordinate unit vector at relabeled index i, and I\nis the identity. R_k is ONLY a proposed comparison profile. It is not the\nactual trajectory of F. The proposed all-query spatial invariant was\n\n    0 <= r_k <= R_k coordinatewise, 0<=k<=ell.         (2)\n\nIt starts at equality r_0=R_0=2z e_0. This is an amplitude and shape\nassertion, stronger than a mass cap or knowledge of the active interval.\nIt retains every original physical step in its comparison kernel.\n\nTo make its quantitative purpose explicit, if (2) held at the terminal\nquery, it would imply\n\n    J_m=N||r_ell||^2 <= 4z^2 N||K_ell||^2.            (3)\n\nThe right side is a linear spatial-kernel quantity. For example, a uniform\nbound N||K_ell||^2<=C would give J_m<=4z^2 C. Finite cyclic Fourier\northogonality gives the exact identity\n\n    N||K_ell||^2\n      =sum_(q=0)^(N-1) product_(j=0)^(m-2)\n           (1-t_j sin^2(pi q/N))^(2^(m-1-j)).         (4)\n\nIndeed the q-th cyclic Fourier vector is an eigenvector of L_e with\neigenvalue4sin^2(pi q/N); expand e_0 in these N orthogonal vectors and\nsquare the eigenvalue product. The multiplicity of t_j in H_(m-1) is\n2^(m-2-j). Formula (4) retains finite-cycle aliasing exactly; it is not an\ninfinite-lattice replacement or an evaluated numerical sum.\n\nWrite A_m=product_(j=0)^(m-2)(1-mu t_j)^(2^(m-2-j)) and\nb_m=A_m(mu d-1)>0. The actual full-word payoff and original benchmark obey\n\n    V_m=2F(x_(2^m-1))/dist(v,argmin F)^2=b_m^2 J_m/(2N^2),\n    B_(2^m-1)V_m=b_m^2 B_(2^m-1) J_m/(2N^2).         (5)\n\nHere B is the ORIGINAL all-split benchmark, defined in accepted\nfact04219554e41d9750 by C_0=B_0=1,\nOmega(s,t)=(s+t+sqrt((s+t)^2+4st))/2,\nG(s,t)=2s+t/2+sqrt(t^2+8st)/2,\nC_n=max_(0<=i<n)Omega(C_i,C_(n-i-1)),\nB_n=max_(0<=i<n)G(C_i,B_(n-i-1)), and U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.\nAll splits remain present. Consequently (2) at the terminal query and\nthe bound C would be strong enough for an eventual deficit whenever, for\nsome fixed delta>0 and all sufficiently large m,\n\n    (2z^2 b_m^2 B_(2^m-1)/N^2) C <= 1-delta.         (6)\n\nConditions (2), the bound C, and (6) are the intended proof obligations,\nnot conclusions. The result below refutes (2) as an all-query invariant.\n\n## 2. Minimal inherited-state and parameter checks\n\nThese checks fix the connection to the original run; static legality and\nthe old first-child analysis are not being offered as new progress.\nDifferentiation gives grad F(x)=D^T(Dx)_+/4, so the actual edge update is\n\n    y' = y-(h/4)L_e y_+,\n    y'_i = y_i-(h/2)(y_i)_+\n                   +(h/4)((y_(i-1))_++(y_(i+1))_+).  (7)\n\nThe gradient is 1-Lipschitz since ||D||<=2 and the positive-part map is\n1-Lipschitz. F vanishes exactly at constant vectors, and the mean is\npreserved. Pairing the coordinates of v gives mean zero; summing\ncos^2((2i+1)theta) using the finite geometric series gives ||v||^2=N/2.\nThus the true squared initial radius is N/2, without changing the declared L.\n\nFor m>=10, the inequalities lambda>12/5 and (12/5)^3>13 imply\n\n    d > (7/8)(12/5)^8 > (7/8)13^2(144/25) > 768.\n\nHence theta^2<=3/(2d)<1/512 and\n\n    0<mu<1/512.                                      (8)\n\nFor completeness let b=pi sqrt(d/6). Since pi>sqrt(6) and sqrt(d)>12,\nb<=N<b+1<(13/12)b. Integration of cos(s)>=1-s^2/2 gives\nsin(theta)/theta>=1-theta^2/6>99/100. Therefore\n\n    5/4 < (3/2)(12/13)^2(99/100)^2 < mu d < 3/2.\n\nAlso t_(m-2)/d=1/c+(1-1/c)/d<5/12+1/768, so\nmu t_j<2/3 for every j<=m-2. Substitution of the cosine addition formulas\nin the active path at v gives grad F(v)=mu v. All factors1-mu t_j in\nthe original first child are positive. Successive physical updates thus\ngive A_m v, followed at the largest step d by -b_m v. Positive homogeneity\nthen shows that the original suffix state after k updates is b_m X_k.\nThis proves the inherited-state assertion and (5); it does not reset a child.\n\nFor the first four actual suffix steps define\n\n    u=t_0/4, w=t_1/4, eta=t_2/4,\n    p=1-2u, q=1-2w=1/(2m).\n\nTheir order is u,w,u,eta in units of h/4. The following bounds hold:\n\n    1/3<u<3/8,       2/7<p<1/3,\n    19/40<=w<1/2,    0<q<=1/20,\n    3/4<eta<7/8,     0<1-beta^2<1/5.                 (9)\n\nTo check the less immediate lower bound on u, write\nt_0=1+((m-1)/(m+1))(sqrt(2)-1). Since (m-1)/(m+1)>=9/11\nand sqrt(2)>38/27, t_0>4/3. The upper bound t_0<sqrt(2) and\nsqrt(2)<10/7 give p>2/7. The other bounds follow from\nt_1=2-1/m and t_2=1+lambda(1-1/m^2), with 3<t_2<7/2.\n\nThere is also an exact relation essential below:\n\n    eta p-(2eta-1)u=(1-beta^2)/4.                    (10)\n\nIndeed (4eta-1)u-eta\n=beta c(1+beta/c)/4-(1+beta c)/4=(beta^2-1)/4.\nThis retains the finite enclosing-m deformation rather than replacing it\nby its limiting Silver parameters.\n\n## 3. The actual inherited profile through the first H_2\n\nAt relabeled edge i, the initial profile is\n\n    y_(0,0)=2z,\n    y_(0,i)=-nu_i, 1<=i<N,\n    nu_i=2sin(theta)sin(2i theta)=nu_(N-i)>0.          (11)\n\nThe exact first boundary depths needed below are\n\n    nu_1=4mu z,\n    nu_2=8mu z(1-2mu),\n    nu_3=mu z(12-64mu+64mu^2),\n    nu_4=16mu z(1-2mu)(1-8mu+8mu^2).                 (12)\n\nThese follow from the sine double-angle formulas. In particular\nnu_2<=8mu z, nu_3<=12mu z, nu_4<=16mu z and\nnu_3>(95/8)mu z by (8).\n\nLet Delta_k=R_k-r_k. For k=1,2,3 all old positive coordinates remain\nstrictly positive: each of these steps has h<2, and (7) retains at least\n(1-h/2) times each old positive value. Only the immediately adjacent\nnegative edges can activate. Their actual candidate values are\n\n    r_(1,1)=2uz-nu_1 > z(2/3-4mu)>0,\n    r_(2,2)=2zuw-(w nu_1+nu_2)\n                              > z(19/60-10mu)>0,\n    r_(3,3)=2zu^2w-u(w nu_1+nu_2)-nu_3\n                              > z(19/180-16mu)>0.   (13)\n\nThe reflected edge indices have equal values. Every other\noutside edge remains at its strictly negative initial value. Since N>27,\nnone of these indices overlaps its reflected counterpart. Induction using\n(13) proves that the strict active set is exactly {-k,...,k} for k=0,1,2,3.\n\nWrite k_i=K_(3,i)=k_(-i). Multiplying the three three-point kernels gives\n\n    k_0=q(p^2+2u^2)+4puw,\n    k_1=2puq+w(p^2+3u^2),\n    k_2=u^2q+2puw,\n    k_3=u^2w,                 k_i=0 for |i|>3.        (14)\n\nFor example, these are the coefficients of\n(p+u(s+s^(-1)))^2(q+w(s+s^(-1))) for a formal variable s.\nThe full symmetric kernel has positive coefficients on its support and\ntotal mass one. At the first birth Delta_1 has value\nnu_1 at edges+1,-1. At the second birth,\n\n    Delta_(2,0)=2w nu_1, Delta_(2,1)=q nu_1,\n    Delta_(2,2)=w nu_1+nu_2.\n\nPropagate these deviations through the next u-step. Subtracting the new\nboundary depth nu_3 from the actual profile adds nu_3 to its deviation\nfrom the free profile. The exact deviations after the original entire H_2 are\n\n    Delta_(3,0)=(2pw+2uq)nu_1,\n    Delta_(3,1)=(pq+3uw)nu_1+u nu_2,\n    Delta_(3,2)=(pw+uq)nu_1+p nu_2,\n    Delta_(3,3)=u(w nu_1+nu_2)+nu_3.                  (15)\n\nThey are reflection invariant and zero off {-3,...,3}. All entries of\nDelta_k for k<=3 are nonnegative, proving (2) through query3. No state is\nreset to an impulse at the end of this H_2: (15) is retained at its bridge.\n\nWe will also use simple bounds following term by term from (9), (12),\nand (14)--(15):\n\n    19/105 < k_0 < 9/32,\n    19/120 < k_1 < 9/32,\n    19/210 < k_2 < 3/20,\n    19/360 < k_3,\n\n    0<=Delta_(3,0)<(3/2)mu z,\n    0<=Delta_(3,1)<6mu z,\n    0<=Delta_(3,2)<(7/2)mu z,\n    0<=Delta_(3,3)<16mu z.                            (16)\n\nFor clarity the upper coefficient bounds before rounding are\nk_0<1553/5760, k_1<1607/5760 and k_2<169/1280.\nThe corresponding deviation bounds before rounding are\n(89/60)mu z, (319/60)mu z, (409/120)mu z and (63/4)mu z.\nThey result respectively from p<1/3, u<3/8, w<1/2, q<=1/20\nand the displayed bounds for nu_i. The lower coefficient bounds use\np>2/7, u>1/3 and w>=19/40.\n\n## 4. Exact envelope failure at the actual t_2 bridge\n\nConsider relabeled edge3. At the incoming query3 it is strictly positive,\nedge2 is strictly positive, and edge4 is strictly negative with positive\npart zero. Also K_(3,4)=0. Thus (7), with the actual inherited profile,\ngives\n\n    y_(4,3)=2z K_(4,3)+E_m,\n    E_m=(2eta-1)Delta_(3,3)-eta Delta_(3,2).           (17)\n\nSubstitute (15), then use (10). The exact excess simplifies to\n\n    E_m=(2eta-1)nu_3\n          -((1-beta^2)/4)(w nu_1+nu_2)-eta u q nu_1,\n\n    E_m/(mu z)\n      =(2eta-1)(12-64mu+64mu^2)\n          -(1-beta^2)(w+2-4mu)-4eta u q. At the corresponding original full-run\nquery, each excess is b_m E_m>5b_m mu z; the true radius, function and\ndeclared smoothness have all remained unchanged.\n\nFinally, as m tends to infinity, mu tends to zero, beta tends to1,\nq tends to0, and eta tends to(2+sqrt(2))/4. Thus kernel positivity through the\ntransition does not imply comparison (2).\n\nThere are no boundary ties in these four steps: every named active\ncoordinate and every unreached negative coordinate has a strict sign.\nThe exact update (7) nevertheless includes ties correctly at arbitrary\nlater times by assigning positive part zero. Hence\n\n    sum_i r_(k,i)=2z-2sum_(j=1)^k nu_j, 0<=k<=4.      (24)\n\nThis is an actual-prefix identity, not a proposed global mass principle.\nIt underscores why a mass cap does not repair the failed pointwise shape\ncomparison.\n\n## 6. Why the absorption comparison fails, and the exact surviving gap\n\nFor an arbitrary actual state y write r=y_+, n=(-y)_+, and U=P_h r.\nIf y_i<=0, then U_i=(h/4)(r_(i-1)+r_(i+1))>=0. Define\n\n    a_i=min(n_i,U_i) if y_i<=0, and a_i=0 otherwise;\n    b_i=(-U_i)_+ if y_i>0, and b_i=0 otherwise.\n\nDirectly taking the positive part of y'=U-n proves\n\n    r_next=P_h r-a+b,      a>=0, b>=0.                (25)\n\nAt an old tie n_i=0, so a_i=0 and the same formula applies. A zero step\nalso satisfies it. The vector b records the repair needed when an old\npositive coordinate becomes strictly negative; strict sign nesting\nimplies b=0. (26)\n\nAll these linear factors are on the same finite cycle; the nonlinear\nsources still depend on the actual chronological states. Original target OPEN.", "evidence": "# Signed cyclic kernels and the unpaid actual source response\n\nStatus: JOINT UNVERIFIED ANALYTICAL DRAFT, max3 round50, 2026-10-01.\nThis is the one analytical round after completed49 authorized by TASK.md and\nmaster_guidance1fc56054c08bd0ac. Completed ceiling50, attempted ceiling51.\nNo numerical evaluation, trajectory replay, solver, symbolic program, benchmark\nDP, fact_submit, installation, consult, or subordinate agent was used.\n\nThe actual terminal comparison remains OPEN. The new kernel estimates and the\nsource obstruction below are draft mathematics, not verifier-accepted facts.\nAll needed ingredients are proved jointly. The two immutable object sources\n37ec6ea9a52bfbdf and66ab91686f1b656f are specifications and awareness, not\nmathematical predecessors. Their SHA256 values were confirmed respectively as\na59effa1d6ff0c1ef15ced41f7eedc4b6fbc9028f5bcd0f5eab1cf5740906a0d and\n3880b0126b514999ca8623850305cdf23cd5cc27bab8d212b590d65e4412493e.\nThe sole accepted mathematical input used below is the unconditional benchmark\nray enclosure in fact04219554e41d9750. No conditional seed is presumed to exist.\n\nThe results of this single signed-kernel mechanism are:\n\n* Every original terminal LINEAR suffix is an exact Euclidean contraction,\n  despite its possibly signed convolution coefficients.\n* For the complete original suffix H_(m-1), its actual N-cycle kernel g obeys\n  N||g||_2^2<241/216, ||g||_1<17/16, and sum_i(-g_i)_+<1/32.\n  Its exact operator norm from l1 to l2 is ||g||_2.\n* Taking absolute values of individual factors instead gives an envelope of\n  mass greater than5^(2^(m-3))/3^(m-1). This quantifies the discarded cancellation.\n* The exact actual nonlinear source response Gamma_m is defined in (25).\n  The actual terminal energy satisfies sqrt(J_m)<17/8+Gamma_m. A proof of\n  limsup Gamma_m<=3 would imply an eventual actual-family benchmark ratio<9/16.\n  That source inequality is NOT proved here.\n* A proposed diagonal squared-source payment already fails on the first TWO\n  actual updates from -Dv. The exact positive excess is (35). It is not a\n  counterexample to the final coherent-source inequality.\n\nThe final missing inequality is written in full in Section10. There is no\nclaim that a stable linear polynomial proves a nonlinear terminal bound.\n\n## 1. The unchanged law and true normalization\n\nFix an integer m>=8, and set\n\n    lambda=1+sqrt(2), beta=1-1/m, c=lambda(1+1/m), a=beta/c,\n    t_j=1+a*c^j, H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,t_j,H_j),\n    M=m-1, ell=2^M-1, n=2^m-1,\n    d=t_(m-1)=1+beta*c^(m-2), N=ceil(pi*sqrt(d/6)),\n    theta=pi/(2N), mu=sin(theta)^2, z=cos(theta),\n    v_i=cos((2i+1)theta), 0<=i<N.                         (1)\n\nAll embedded words use this SAME enclosing m. The cycle has the directed edges\ni to i+1 modulo N, all of weight one. On real vertex vectors set\n\n    (D x)_i=x_i-x_(i+1), L_e=D D^T,\n    F(x)=||(D x)_+||_2^2/8,\n    T_t(x)=x-(t/4)D^T(Dx)_+.                            (2)\n\nPositive and negative parts are coordinatewise: u_+=max(u,0), u_-=(-u)_+.\nThe norm ||.||_p is the ordinary finite-dimensional lp norm; inner products\nare Euclidean. An identity matrix is denoted I. The declared smoothness is1.\n\nIndeed, the derivative of u_+^2/2 is u_+, and hence\ngrad F(x)=D^T(Dx)_+/4. Since ||D||<=2 by summing\n(x_i-x_(i+1))^2<=2x_i^2+2x_(i+1)^2, and positive part is1-Lipschitz,\ngrad F is1-Lipschitz. Convexity follows from the scalar summands. The minimum\nis zero, and F(x)=0 implies x_i<=x_(i+1) all around the cycle, so its full\nminimizer set is span{1}. The gradient has zero sum and every update preserves\nthe mean. Pairing i with N-1-i proves sum_i v_i=0. The sine-difference identity\n\n    2sin(pi/N)cos((2i+1)pi/N)\n      =sin((2i+2)pi/N)-sin(2i*pi/N)\n\nand cos^2 u=(1+cos(2u))/2 give\n\n    R^2=dist(v,argmin F)^2=||v||_2^2=N/2.               (3)\n\nThe full original trajectory starts at v and follows H_m. No amplitude,\nsmoothness, radius, graph, or step schedule is changed below.\n\nFor completeness every original directed interpolation row is retained. For\nany x,w, with r=(Dx)_+,s=(Dw)_+, scalar sign checking gives\n\n F(x)-F(w)+<grad F(x),w-x>+||grad F(x)-grad F(w)||_2^2/2\n   =-sum_i r_i(-(Dw)_i)_+/4\n    -sum_i[(r_i-s_i)+(r_(i+1)-s_(i+1))]^2/32<=0.       (4)\n\nTo check (4), start from\nphi(u)-phi(v)+u_+(v-u)+(u_+-v_+)^2/2=-u_+(-v)_+,\nwhere phi(u)=u_+^2/2. Sum at u=(Dx)_i/2,v=(Dw)_i/2 and subtract the\ngradient-norm correction. The identity\n<q,(I-L_e/4)q>=sum_i(q_i+q_(i+1))^2/4 gives the displayed result.\nThis covers ties, nonlocal pairs, both terminal directions and optimum rows.\n\n## 2. Inherited suffix and elementary parameter bounds\n\nWe will use\n\n  12/5<c<11/4<3, 1/4<a<1/2, 7/8<=beta<1,\n  1<t_0<3/2, t_1=2-1/m<2, t_2=1+lambda(1-1/m^2)>3.   (5)\n\nFor the upper bound on c, lambda<22/9 and1+1/m<=9/8 suffice.\nThe lower bound on a follows from a=(m-1)/((m+1)lambda)>=7/(9lambda)>7/27>1/4.\nThe remaining bounds follow directly from (1), lambda>12/5, and lambda>2.\n\nAlso\n\n    d>144, N>12, theta<1/8, mu<1/96,\n    5/4<mu*d<3/2, mu*t_j<2/3 for j<=m-2.              (6)\n\nHere d>1+(7/8)(12/5)^6>1+(7/8)13^2>144. Put b=pi*sqrt(d/6).\nSince pi>sqrt(6), b>sqrt(d)>12 and\nb<=N<b+1<(13/12)b. Thus theta^2<1/96. Integration of\ncos u>=1-u^2/2 gives sin(theta)/theta>=1-theta^2/6>99/100. It follows that\nmu*d<3/2 and mu*d>(3/2)(12/13)^2(99/100)^2>5/4;\nthe last comparison is2117016>2112500. Finally\nt_(m-2)/d=1/c+(1-1/c)/d<5/12+1/144=61/144, giving the last bound.\n\nOn the positive ray through v the active edges are0,...,N-2. Cosine addition\nat interior vertices and at the two path endpoints gives grad F(v)=mu*v.\nThe last inequality in (6) therefore proves, by induction on the actual\nphysical updates in the first child, that\n\n    A_m=product_(j=0)^(m-2)(1-mu*t_j)^(2^(m-2-j))>0,\n    x_after_first_child=A_m*v,\n    x_after_central_step=-b_m*v,\n    b_m=A_m*(mu*d-1)>0.                                (7)\n\nThe multiplicities follow from the recursive word, and the induction justifies\nregrouping these scalar factors only during this first, unchanged sector.\n\nNormalize the actual inherited suffix by its positive factor b_m: let X_0=-v\nand let X_k follow H_M using (2). Define actual edge states\n\n    y_k=D X_k, r_k=(y_k)_+, n_k=(-y_k)_+,\n    h_k=(H_M)_k=t_(nu_2(k+1)), 0<=k<ell,\n    J_m=N||r_ell||_2^2.                                (8)\n\nHere nu_2(u) is the nonnegative integer power of2 dividing the positive\ninteger u. The formula for h_k follows by induction on the two children and\ntheir central index. Positive homogeneity of (2) implies that the actual full\nterminal point is b_m*X_ell. Its normalized value is exactly\n\n    V_m=2F(b_m*X_ell)/R^2=b_m^2*J_m/(2N^2).             (9)\n\nThis normalization does not reset a child state. The actual normalized suffix\nhas r_0=2z*e_(N-1), where e_i denotes the edge-coordinate unit vector.\n\n## 3. Real cyclic polynomials and exact aliasing\n\nLet S be the cyclic shift on edge vectors, (S f)_i=f_(i-1). Direct multiplication\nof D and D^T yields L_e=2I-S-S^(-1). Consequently\n\n    P_t=I-(t/4)L_e=p_t(S),\n    p_t(w)=1-t/2+(t/4)(w+w^(-1)).                      (10)\n\nThe indeterminate w is unrelated to any state. All products are in the REAL\nLaurent-polynomial ring modulo w^N-1. If an ordinary Laurent product has\ncoefficients c_q, -L<=q<=L, its actual N-cycle kernel is exactly\n\n    g_i=sum_(q congruent i mod N)c_q, 0<=i<N.           (11)\n\nFormula (11) sums every alias, including repeated windings. No relation\nbetween N and a power of2 is assumed. Multiplication becomes cyclic\nconvolution, (g*f)_i=sum_j g_j f_(i-j), so aliases are taken after every\nproduct or after the full product with identical results. For a new factor\nthe cyclic coefficients obey\n\n    g'_i=(1-t/2)g_i+(t/4)(g_(i-1)+g_(i+1)).             (12)\n\nEquation (12) is an identity for LINEAR kernels, not the positive-part state\nupdate. Its central coefficient may be negative.\n\nFor any original chronological interval [u,v) of physical indices in H_M,\nthe exact multiplicity of level j is\n\n c_j[u,v)=floor(v/2^j)-floor(u/2^j)\n           -floor(v/2^(j+1))+floor(u/2^(j+1)).          (13)\n\nIndeed it counts the integers u<q<=v divisible by2^j but not2^(j+1).\nIts linear Laurent product is product_j p_(t_j)(w)^(c_j[u,v)). This regrouping\nis valid because all P_t are polynomials in the same shift. It does not\ncommute or rearrange the nonlinear physical maps.\n\nFor 0<=l<N set xi_l=sin^2(pi*l/N). The characters\nf_i=exp(2pi*sqrt(-1)*l*i/N) have L_e/4 eigenvalue xi_l. Define the unnormalized\nFourier transform by hat f_l=sum_i f_i exp(-2pi*sqrt(-1)*l*i/N).\nThe finite geometric series gives character orthogonality, inversion with\nfactor1/N, and\n\n    N||f||_2^2=sum_l|hat f_l|^2.                        (14)\n\nThe multiplier of (13) is product_j(1-t_j*xi_l)^(c_j[u,v)). Thus the exact\nnorm of the corresponding operator from l2 to l2 is the largest multiplier\nmodulus. If g is its coefficient vector, its exact l1-to-l2 norm is ||g||_2:\ntriangle inequality applied to shifted copies of g gives the upper bound,\nand a unit impulse attains it. These identities already include (11).\nThey involve real coefficients and complex characters only as a basis;\nno finite-field inference is used.\n\n## 4. A uniform scalar bound for every complete child\n\nDefine, for real x in[0,1],\n\n    q_k(x)=product_(j=0)^(k-1)(1-t_j*x)^(2^(k-1-j)),\n    q_0(x)=1, q_(k+1)(x)=q_k(x)^2*(1-t_k*x),\n    S_k=sum H_k.                                      (15)\n\nThe symbols q_k and S_k in this draft are scalar child products and physical\nword sums. In particular B_n is reserved for the original benchmark.\n\nFor every k>=0 and0<x<=1 we claim\n\n    |q_k(x)|<=min{1,1/(beta*c^k*x)}.                   (16)\n\nFirst S_1=1+a>4beta/3=(4/3)a*c, because c<3 and beta<1. If\nS_k>=(4/3)a*c^k, then\nS_(k+1)=2S_k+1+a*c^k>=(11/3)a*c^k+1>(4/3)a*c^(k+1),\nusing c<11/4. Thus S_k>=(4/3)a*c^k for every k>=1. With e=exp(1), set\n\n    kappa=3c/(4e)<99/128<4/5,                         (17)\n\nwhere e>8/3. We prove (16) by three exhaustive sign cases, with exact roots\ngiving q_k=0 and k=0 giving the claim directly.\n\nIf all t_j*x<1 for j<k, then 1-u<=exp(-u) gives\n0<q_k(x)<=exp(-S_k*x)<=1/(e*S_k*x)<=kappa/(beta*c^k*x),\nas well as q_k<=1. The maximum of u exp(-u) is1/e, by differentiation.\n\nIf t_0*x>1, then all factors are negative and\n|1-t_j*x|=a*c^j*x-(1-x)<=a*c^j*x. The multiplicity identities\n\n    sum_(j<k)2^(k-1-j)=2^k-1,\n    sum_(j<k)j*2^(k-1-j)=2^k-k-1\n\nfollow by induction. They give\n|q_k(x)|<=(beta*x)^(2^k-1)*c^(-k). This is at most1, and after multiplying\nby beta*c^k*x it is at most(beta*x)^(2^k)<=1.\n\nIn the remaining case let J>=1 be the first index with t_J*x>1, and put\np=k-J>=1. All factors in q_J are positive. Using the same multiplicity\nidentities on the tail and |1-t_j*x|<=a*c^j*x there gives\n\n |q_k(x)|\n   <=q_J(x)^(2^p)*(a*c^J*x)^(2^p-1)*c^(2^p-p-1)\n   =[a*c^(J+1)*x*q_J(x)]^(2^p)/(beta*c^k*x)\n   <=kappa^(2^p)/(beta*c^k*x).                         (18)\n\nFor the final inequality, q_J<=exp(-S_J*x) and\na*c^(J+1)*x*exp(-S_J*x)<=a*c^(J+1)/(e*S_J)<=kappa.\nAlso x<1/t_(J-1)<=1/(1+a), whereas t_J*x>1 implies\na*c^J*x>1-x>a/(1+a)>1/5. Consequently\nbeta*c^k*x>c^(p+1)/5>=c^2/5>144/125>1.\nThis proves both parts of (16) in the remaining case. Values at endpoints\nand exact roots follow directly or by continuity.\n\nThe stronger factor kappa^(2^p) in (18) remains available. No ordering of\nnonlinear states or positive kernel coefficient is needed for this estimate.\n\n## 5. Binary terminal suffixes and exact internal contraction\n\nDefine the block multiplier\n\n    b_j(x)=(1-t_j*x)q_j(x).                            (19)\n\nIt represents the actual block (t_j,H_j) of length2^j. If t_j*x<=1,\n(16) gives |b_j(x)|<=1. If t_j*x>1, then\n|1-t_j*x|<=a*c^j*x and (16) give |b_j(x)|<=1/c<1.\nThe same argument works for j=0, since q_0=1 and beta*x<1.\nAt x=0 every b_j equals1.\n\nWrite a remaining length R_rem,0<=R_rem<=2^M-1, in binary. The terminal suffix of\nH_M of length R_rem is the concatenation of (t_j,H_j) over its nonzero binary\ndigits, in INCREASING j. To prove this, if R_rem<2^(M-1), the suffix is within\nthe final H_(M-1) and induction applies. If R_rem=2^(M-1)+r, its last block is\n(t_(M-1),H_(M-1)), preceded by a terminal length-r suffix of the first\nH_(M-1). This is exactly the next nonzero binary digit. The empty case starts\nthe induction. In particular the product of all blocks j<k is q_k.\n\nLet k_R_rem(x)=product_(j:bit_j(R_rem)=1)b_j(x), including k_0=1, and let K_R_rem be its\nactual N-cycle operator. The preceding proof gives\n\n    |k_R_rem(x)|<=1 on[0,1], ||K_R_rem||_(2 to2)=1.    (20)\n\nEquality of the norm follows from the constant vector. This is an exact sharp\noperator bound for EVERY terminal linear suffix. It does not assert l1 or\norder-preserving contraction, and it does not apply (20) to nonlinear maps.\n\nThere is also a bounded Laplacian-weighted internal factor. After an actual\nstep of level j, the remaining word begins with H_j, followed by some higher\nblocks. Hence its multiplier contains q_j times factors of modulus<=1.\nUsing (16), for every x in[0,1],\n\n t_j*x*|k_(ell-k-1)(x)|\n   <=t_j*x*|q_j(x)|<=1/c+1/(beta*c^j)\n   <5/12+8/7=131/84<8/5,\n       when h_k=t_j.                                 (21)\n\nThis controls an individual term in the summation-by-parts formula below;\nit is not a bound on the sum of actual negative-part states.\n\n## 6. Full-kernel smoothing, small negative mass, and lost cancellation\n\nLet g be the kernel of K_ell, the full H_M linear product. The actual rounding\nlaw gives, using (6), pi^2<10 and c>12/5,\n\n N^2/(beta*c^M)\n   <[169*pi^2/(864*c)]*d/(d-1)\n   <8450/10296<5/6.                                   (22)\n\nFor 1<=l<N put q=min(l,N-l). Concavity of sine on[0,pi/2] gives\nsin(pi*q/N)>=2q/N. From (16), |q_M(xi_l)|<5/(24q^2).\nAt frequency0 the multiplier is1. There are at most two frequencies for\neach q. Since sum_(q>=1)q^(-4)<1+integral_1^infinity u^(-4)du=4/3,\n(14) yields\n\n    N||g||_2^2=sum_l|q_M(xi_l)|^2<241/216,\n    ||g||_1<=sqrt(N)||g||_2<sqrt(241/216)<17/16,\n    sum_i(-g_i)_+=(||g||_1-1)/2<1/32.                 (23)\n\nThe last identity uses sum_i g_i=q_M(0)=1. It permits signed coefficients;\nit neither asserts nor requires any negative coefficient actually to occur.\nIn particular, the exact linear propagation of the actual positive initial\npart has\n\n    N||K_ell*r_0||_2^2=4z^2*N||g||_2^2<241/54,\n    sqrt(N)||K_ell*r_0||_2<17/8.                       (24)\n\nFor comparison, take absolute values of every individual factor in (10)\nbefore multiplying. Its nonnegative envelope has total mass1 for t<=2,\nand t-1 for t>2. Using (5), the full envelope's mass for H_M is exactly\n\n product_(j=2)^(M-1)(t_j-1)^(2^(M-1-j))\n   =(beta*c^2)^(2^(M-2))/(beta*c^M)\n   >5^(2^(M-2))/3^M.\n\nThe count of the exponents is2^(M-2)-1 and their j-weighted sum is\n3*2^(M-2)-M-1; substitution of t_j-1=a*c^j gives the identity. Finally\nbeta*c^2>(7/8)(12/5)^2=126/25>5 and beta*c^M<3^M prove the strict bound.\nSince the envelope coefficients are nonnegative, folding onto the ACTUAL\ncycle by (11) leaves this mass unchanged. In terms of m the last lower bound\nis5^(2^(m-3))/3^(m-1), whereas the signed kernel itself has mass norm<17/16.\nAn estimate based on that product of absolute factors loses this much\ncancellation even before any nonlinear source is priced.\n\n## 7. Exact actual sources: signs, support, amplitudes and Duhamel\n\nAt step k put alpha=h_k/4 and w_i=alpha(r_(k,i-1)+r_(k,i+1)). The original\npositive-part edge update, obtained by applying D to (2), is\n\n    y_(k+1,i)=y_(k,i)-2alpha*r_(k,i)+w_i.\n\nDefine the absorption vector abs_k and the repair vector rep_k by\n\n abs_(k,i)=1_(y_(k,i)<=0)*min(n_(k,i),w_i),\n rep_(k,i)=1_(y_(k,i)>0)*[(2alpha-1)r_(k,i)-w_i]_+,\n s_k=rep_k-abs_k.\n\nBoth vectors are nonnegative and their supports are disjoint. Absorption\noccurs only on old strictly negative edges adjacent to an old positive edge;\nat an old zero its amplitude is zero. Its amplitude is at most both the\nold negative depth n_(k,i) and the incoming amount w_i. Repair occurs only\non old positive edges that become strictly negative. It vanishes if h_k<=2,\nand satisfies rep_(k,i)<=max(h_k/2-1,0)r_(k,i). Thus no large-step increase\nor sign change has been omitted. Exact ties give zero on the appropriate\nboundary and require no choice of active-set convention.\n\nChecking the two old sign cases gives the identities\n\n    r_(k+1)=P_(h_k)r_k+s_k,\n    n_(k+1)=n_k+s_k.\n\nOn an old nonpositive coordinate P r=w>=0 and r'=(w-n)_+=w-min(w,n).\nOn an old positive coordinate n=0 and taking the positive part adds exactly\n(-P r)_+. This proves both identities, including ties.\n\nThere is useful local dissipativity: writing v_lin=P_(h_k)r_k, we have v_lin=-rep_k\non the repair support and v_lin>=abs_k on the absorption support. Therefore\n\n ||r_(k+1)||_2^2-||v_lin||_2^2\n   =-||rep_k||_2^2-2<v_lin,abs_k>+||abs_k||_2^2\n   <=-||rep_k||_2^2-||abs_k||_2^2.\n\nThis does not assert ||v_lin||<=||r_k|| at large steps. Also sum y_k=0 at all\ntimes, so if M_k=sum_i r_(k,i), then\nM_(k+1)-M_k=sum rep_k-sum abs_k. The repair term is not set to zero.\n\nIterating the actual affine-in-r identity, retaining each source where its\nactual state generates it, gives\n\n r_ell=K_ell*r_0+sum_(k=0)^(ell-1)K_(ell-k-1)*s_k,\n Gamma_m=sqrt(N)||sum_(k=0)^(ell-1)K_(ell-k-1)*s_k||_2.\n                                                               (25)\n\nThe multiplication symbol here denotes an operator acting on a vector, or\nequivalently convolution with its cyclic kernel. In particular the last\nsource has the IDENTITY propagator K_0. No source moves to a different time.\nBy (24) and triangle inequality this gives the unconditional actual estimate\n\n    sqrt(J_m)<17/8+Gamma_m.                            (26)\n\nThis is a paid linear term plus an EXPLICIT unpaid nonlinear source term.\nFor clarity the entirely resolved Fourier expression for that source term is\n\n Gamma_m^2=sum_(l=0)^(N-1)\n    |sum_(k=0)^(ell-1) k_(ell-k-1)(xi_l)\n              [hat rep_(k,l)-hat abs_(k,l)]|^2.         (27)\n\nThe inner sums retain all source phases and signs. Their kernels are fixed\npolynomials from (15),(19); only the actual trajectory generates the sources.\n\nThere are two useful exact alternative ways to inspect the missing payment.\nFirst, expanding the square in (25) includes every term\n<s_j,K_(ell-j-1)K_(ell-k-1)s_k>. Although these matrices commute and are\nsymmetric, these cross terms cannot be discarded; Section8 gives an actual\ncounterexample. Second, s_k=n_(k+1)-n_k gives summation by parts:\n\n sum_k K_(ell-k-1)*s_k\n   =n_ell-K_ell*n_0\n       -sum_k (h_k/4)L_e K_(ell-k-1)*n_k.              (28)\n\nFor verification, the direct telescoping has endpoint -K_(ell-1)n_0 and\ninterior differences K_(ell-k)-K_(ell-k-1)\n=-(h_k/4)L_e K_(ell-k-1). Adding the k=0 term converts the endpoint to\n-K_ell n_0. At frequency zero (28) is exactly M_ell-M_0, since\nsum n_k=sum r_k. At other frequencies (21) bounds each weighted operator\nbut provides no cancellation or summability of its entire actual history.\nEquations (27) and (28) identify the missing information without changing\nthe physical chronology.\n\n## 8. Two direct source-payment tests and their precise failures\n\n### 8.1 Diagonal square payment fails on the prescribed actual prefix\n\nOne tempting repair of (27) is to use contraction of all propagators to claim\nthe squared response is bounded by the sum of squared source norms. Even the\nfirst two actual physical steps from -Dv refute this proposed general lemma.\n\nRelabel the closing edge N-1 as spatial0 and its neighbors by signed indices.\nThis changes labels only. Write u=t_0/4,w=t_1/4. From (5),(6),\n\n    1/4<u<3/8, 15/32<=w<1/2, mu<1/96.\n\nCosine subtraction for the SAME initial vector gives\n\n r_0=2z e_0, y_(0,+-1)=-4mu z,\n y_(0,+-2)=-8mu z(1-2mu).\n\nAll other noncentral initial edges are negative. The first step leaves the\ncentral edge positive and activates exactly its two neighbors, with\nr_(1,+-1)=z(2u-4mu)>0. Therefore\n\n    abs_0=A(e_1+e_-1), rep_0=0, A=4mu z.              (29)\n\nAt the second step all three old positives stay positive because w<1/2.\nThe next two edges activate, since\n\n w(2u-4mu)>(15/32)(11/24)=165/768>1/12\n                      >8mu(1-2mu).\n\nAll other edges have no positive neighbor and remain unchanged and negative.\nThus the exact second correction is\n\n    abs_1=C(e_2+e_-2), rep_1=0,\n    C=8mu z(1-2mu)=2A(1-2mu).                         (30)\n\nLet E=e_1+e_-1. The entries of P_(t1)E are1-2w at+-1, w at+-2, and2w at0.\nN>12 prevents any alias collision among these named coordinates. Since\ns_0=-abs_0,s_1=-abs_1, we obtain\n\n ||P_(t1)s_0||_2^2=2A^2(1-4w+7w^2),\n <P_(t1)s_0,s_1>=2ACw,\n ||s_0||_2^2=2A^2.                                   (31)\n\nThe exact excess in the attempted squared-source payment is consequently\n\n ||P_(t1)s_0+s_1||_2^2-||s_0||_2^2-||s_1||_2^2\n    =2A^2*w*(7w-8mu)>0.                              (32)\n\nThe strict inequality follows from7w>=105/32 and8mu<1/12. Both step factors\nare genuine Euclidean contractions, because t_0,t_1<2 and their multipliers\nare1-t_j*xi_l with0<=xi_l<=1. The local source supports are disjoint, but\npropagation makes their negative absorptions overlap coherently.\n\nThis is a counterexample on the actual prescribed start and original first\ntwo steps, not a substitute diagnostic state. Its scope is the generic\ndiagonal source-payment lemma for actual prefixes. The final-horizon weights\nin (27) are different, so (32) does NOT disprove limsup Gamma_m<=3.\n\n### 8.2 An absolute source budget needs the actual time-dependent prices\n\nFor a terminal length R_rem define the EXACT nonnegative linear price\n\n    gamma_R_rem=[sum_l|k_R_rem(xi_l)|^2]^(1/2)\n            =sqrt(N)||kernel(K_R_rem)||_2.\n\nThe l1-to-l2 identity in Section3 gives the rigorous sufficient bound\n\n    Gamma_m<=sum_(k=0)^(ell-1)\n       gamma_(ell-k-1)(||abs_k||_1+||rep_k||_1).        (33)\n\nThis bound loses cancellation between source times, but not cancellation\ninside the real kernel. Unlike a claim with unspecified constants, it makes\neach price explicit. Replacing every gamma_R_rem by the full-kernel bound17/16\nis false on these very same physical suffixes. In fact gamma_0=sqrt(N).\nThe length-one terminal suffix is the original t_0, and\n\n gamma_1^2=N[(1-t_0/2)^2+2(t_0/4)^2]\n            =N[1-t_0+3t_0^2/8]>=N/3,                 (34)\n\nbecause the bracket is minimized at t_0=4/3 with value1/3. Thus the final\nabsorption source and its predecessor need an actual amplitude or cancellation\nestimate at their actual late times. The exact sources (29),(30) do not give\nsuch an estimate for those different inherited late states.\n\nThe alternative use of (20) gives only\nGamma_m<=sqrt(N)sum_k||s_k||_2. Its proposed diagonal improvement is refuted\nby the ACTUAL strict inequality\n\n N||P_(t1)s_0+s_1||_2^2\n   -N(||s_0||_2^2+||s_1||_2^2)=2NA^2*w*(7w-8mu)>0.   (35)\n\nFinally the summation-by-parts attempt (28) retains a different obstacle:\nthe bounded individual weights (21) are multiplied by all actual n_k,\nincluding newly created negative depths at oversteps. Neither an assumed\nabsence of those depths nor a sum estimate follows from (20). No scalar\ntotal-clock/mass-cap closure is retried here. The two direct screens locate\nthe source cross terms and the late absorption amplitudes that are still\nuncontrolled within this same mechanism.\n\n## 9. The exact all-split benchmark and a quantitative source threshold\n\nWe retain the original benchmark, in the W=2R normalization:\n\n C_0=B_0=1,\n Omega(s,t)=(s+t+sqrt((s+t)^2+4st))/2,\n G(s,t)=2s+t/2+sqrt(t^2+8st)/2,\n C_n=max_(0<=j<n)Omega(C_j,C_(n-j-1)),\n B_n=max_(0<=j<n)G(C_j,B_(n-j-1)), U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.\n\nAll split indices remain in these definitions. Accepted fact04219554e41d9750,\nits unconditional statement and proof Section7, gives\nB_(2^m-1)/lambda^m -> Z_1 with0<Z_1<infinity. Its enclosure at b=1,j=0,\nand B_1=4 from the original recurrence, give\n\n    Z_1<=(2+3/100)^2=41209/10000<21/5.                (36)\n\nWe also need the incoming amplitude asymptotics; these are rederived rather\nthan imported from an unverified draft. From (1),\n\n d/lambda^m -> exp(1)/lambda^2,\n N^2/lambda^m -> pi^2*exp(1)/(6lambda^2), mu*d ->3/2.\n\nThe first follows by taking logarithms of\n(1-1/m)(1+1/m)^(m-2). Bounds s-s^2/2<=log(1+s)<=s show its limit is e.\nThe additive1 in d and the ceiling error in N are negligible. The elementary\nlimit sin(theta)/theta->1 proves the last assertion.\n\nReindex (7) by r=m-1-j to write\n\n A_m=product_(r=1)^(m-1)\n      [1-mu-(mu*d-mu)c^(-r)]^(2^(r-1)).\n\nEvery subtracted quantity lies in[0,2/3) by (6), and\n-log(1-u)<=3u in this interval by integration. Objects, accepted input, and the proposed theorem\n\nAll vectors and matrices below are real unless exact field data are specified.\nWrite q58=sqrt(2), lambda=1+q58, u=q58-1=1/lambda, and\n\n    hbar=(q58,2,q58,1+lambda,q58,2,q58),  Abar=lambda^3.\n\nFor h=(h0,...,h6) in [0,infinity)^7, let s=sum_i h_i and A(h)=1+s.\nLet e be the all-ones column in R8, and let e_i be its coordinate columns,\nindexed0,...,7. For any zero-diagonal real8-by8 array X define\n\n    L(X)=diag(Xe)-X.\n\nThe prefix matrix T(h) has diagonal1, entry h_j at i,j when j<i, and zero\nabove its diagonal. Put\n\n    omega(h)=(-2h0,...,-2h6,2s)^T,\n    D(h)=diag(h0(2-h0),...,h6(2-h6),-s(s+2)),\n    M(h,Lambda)=D(h)+sym(L(Lambda)T(h)),\n    sym(K)=(K+K^T)/2.\n\nA full certificate means precisely\n\n    Lambda_ii=0, Lambda_ij>=0 for ALL ordered i!=j,\n    L(Lambda)^T e=omega(h), M(h,Lambda)>=0 on the ENTIRE R8.
